	country_event = { #决定谁领导至上党
		id = TEX.18
		immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event TEX.18"}
		title = TEX.18.t
		desc = TEX.18.d
		picture = GFX_TEX_18

		is_triggered_only = yes
		fire_only_once = yes

		option = { 
			trigger = {
				check_variable = { TEX_smith_power > 60 }
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = TEX_smith_out
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = TEX_smith_with_coughlin
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = TEX_smith_power
						value = TEX_long_power
						compare = greater_than
					}
				}
				else = {
					check_variable = {
						var = TEX_smith_power
						value = TEX_long_power
						compare = greater_than
					}		
					check_variable = {
						var = TEX_smith_power
						value = TEX_coughlin_power
						compare = greater_than
					}		
				}
			}			
			name = TEX.18.a
			promote_character = {
				character = TEX_Gerald_L_K_Smith
				ideology = christian_crusade_subtype
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = paternal_autocrat
				long_name = TEX_smith_party_long
				name = TEX_smith_party
			}
			set_politics = {
				ruling_party = paternal_autocrat
				election_frequency = 48
				elections_allowed = no
			}			
			set_country_flag = TEX_smith_victory
			set_cosmetic_tag = TEX_smith
			hidden_effect = {
				remove_all_ministers = yes	
				add_ideas = TEX_John_Overton_hog_pau_1
				add_ideas = TEX_W_A_Criswell_for_pau_1
				add_ideas = TEX_Orval_Faubus_eco_ade_1
				add_ideas = TEX_Meade_McClanahan_sec_pau_1
			}
		}
		option = { 
			trigger = {
				check_variable = { TEX_coughlin_power > 60 }
				NOT = {
					has_country_flag = TEX_coughlin_out
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = TEX_bingham_survey
					}
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_country_flag = TEX_bingham_out
						}
						if = {
							has_country_flag = TEX_smith_with_coughlin
						}
						check_variable = {
							var = TEX_coughlin_power
							value = TEX_long_power
							compare = greater_than
						}
						else = {
							check_variable = {
								var = TEX_coughlin_power
								value = TEX_long_power
								compare = greater_than
							}		
							check_variable = {
								var = TEX_coughlin_power
								value = TEX_smith_power
								compare = greater_than
							}							
						}
					}
					else = {
						check_variable = {
							var = TEX_coughlin_power
							value = TEX_long_power
							compare = greater_than
						}			
						check_variable = {
							var = TEX_coughlin_power
							value = TEX_bingham_power
							compare = greater_than
						}							
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						has_country_flag = TEX_smith_with_coughlin
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = TEX_coughlin_power
						value = TEX_long_power
						compare = greater_than
					}		
				}
				else = {
					check_variable = {
						var = TEX_coughlin_power
						value = TEX_long_power
						compare = greater_than
					}		
					check_variable = {
						var = TEX_coughlin_power
						value = TEX_smith_power
						compare = greater_than
					}		
				}
			}	
			name = TEX.18.b
			promote_character = {
				character = TEX_Charles_Coughlin
				ideology = longism_subtype
			}
			set_party_name = {
				ideology = authoritarian_democrat
				long_name = TEX_authoritarian_democrat_party_long
				name = TEX_authoritarian_democrat_party

	country_event = { # Socialist terrorism - factory sabotaged
		id = unionstate.200
		immediate = {log = "[GetDateText]: [Root.GetName]: event unionstate.200"}
		title = unionstate.200.t
		desc = unionstate.200.d
		picture = GFX_report_event_SRI_bombing

		trigger = {
			tag = TEX
			OR = {
				has_idea = ACW_socialist_resistance_1
				has_idea = ACW_socialist_resistance_2
				has_idea = ACW_socialist_resistance_3
				has_idea = ACW_socialist_resistance_4
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				OR = {
					is_core_of = USA
					is_core_of = CSA
					is_core_of = TEX
					is_core_of = CAL
				}
				industrial_complex > 0
				is_controlled_by = TEX
			}
		}

		mean_time_to_happen = {
			days = 45
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.25
				has_idea = ACW_socialist_resistance_2
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 1.5
				has_idea = ACW_socialist_resistance_3
			}
			modifier = {
				factor = 2.0
				has_idea = ACW_socialist_resistance_4
			}
		}

		immediate = {
			hidden_effect = {
				random_owned_state = {
					limit = {
						OR = {
							is_core_of = USA
							is_core_of = CSA
							is_core_of = TEX
							is_core_of = CAL
						}
						industrial_complex > 0
						is_controlled_by = TEX
					}
					save_event_target_as = attack_state
				}
			}
		}

		option = {
			name = unionstate.200.a
			add_stability = -0.01
			event_target:attack_state = {
				damage_building = {
					type = industrial_complex
					damage = 1.0
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						arms_factory > 0
					}
					random_list = {
						50 = {
							event_target:attack_state = {
								damage_building = {
									type = arms_factory
									damage = 1.0
								}
							}
						}
						50 = {}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						infrastructure > 3
					}
					random_list = {
						25 = {
							event_target:attack_state = {
								damage_building = {
									type = infrastructure
									damage = 2.0
								}
							}
						}
						50 = {
							event_target:attack_state = {
								damage_building = {
									type = infrastructure
									damage = 1.0
								}
							}
						}
						25 = {}
					}
				}
				random_list = {
					25 = {
						ROOT = {
							add_manpower = -3000
						}
					}
					50 = {
						ROOT = {
							add_manpower = -1500
						}
					}
					25 = {
						ROOT = {
							add_manpower = -500
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}

					factor = 0

					}
				}
			}

					id = unionstatefp.2

				}
			}
			
		}
		option = { #no

				factor = 25
				modifier = {
					factor = 0

						rule = TEX_foreign_policy

					}
				}
